! Title: AdBlock DNS Lite
! Description: 适用于 AdGuard、AdGuardHome 的去广告合并规则，每 8 个小时更新一次。规则源：AdGuard Base filter、AdGuard Chinese filter、AdGuard Mobile Ads filter、AdGuard DNS filter、EasyList、EasyList China、EasyPrivacy、CJX's Annoyance List、xinggsf rule、xinggsf mv、jiekouAD、anti-AD、ADgk、大圣净化 Purple、AdRules DNS List、Hagezi Light、Hagezi Pro、Hagezi Ultimate、Hagezi Threat Intelligence、Hagezi Anti-Piracy、Hagezi Xiaomi、Hagezi Apple、Hagezi Huawei、Hagezi TikTok、1Hosts Lite、OISD Full、AWAvenue Ads Rule、Hblock、BlockListProject Ads、BlockListProject Tracking、Peter Lowe、AdAway、GoodbyeAds、1024_hosts、jiekouAD、秋风广告过滤规则、AdGuard Allowlist。Lite 版仅针对国内域名拦截。
